1b1('bum): @##//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa stong phrag brgya pa/bam 
1b2('bum): po brgya dang gya dgu pa/gsol pa bcom ldan 'das gang de ltar rtogs pa 
1b3('bum): ni|byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i shes rab kyi pha rol tu 
1b4('bum): phyin pa'o//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rab 'byor shin tu 
1b5('bum): rnam par dag pa'i phyir ro//gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das ci'i slad du/
1b6('bum): 'du byed rnam dag don yod grub/
2a1('bum): @##//gang de ltar rtogs pa de byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i shes rab kyi pha rol tu 
2a2('bum): phyin pa lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid kyi phyir ro//gsol pa/
2a3('bum): bcom ldan 'das gal te/byang chub sems dpa' sems/dpa' chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa'i 
2a4('bum): tshe/tshul rol gyi 'gram 'am/pha rol gyi 'gram 'am/gnyis kyi dbus na mi gnas na de ni/byang chub sems dpa' 
2a5('bum): sems dpa' chen po'i shes rib kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka'-i stsal pa/rab 'byor shin 
2a6('bum): tu rnam par dag pa'i phyir ro//gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das ci'i slad du/byang chub sems dpa' chen po 
2b1('bum): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa'i tshe/tshul rol gyi 'gram 'am/pha rol gyi 'gram 'am/gnyis kyi dbus na mi gnas shing /shin tu rnam par dag pa lags/    
2b2('bum): bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal ba/rab 'byor dus gsum mnyam pa nyid kyi phyir ro//gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das gal te/byang chub sems dpa'i theg pa pa'i rigs kyi 
2b3('bum): bu 'am/rigs kyi bu mo thabs mi mkhas pas shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa 'di la/dmigs pa'i tshul du 'du shes su bgyid pa ni/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinab gsog 
2b4('bum): tu 'byin pa'o//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na ba ring du spong ba'o//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka'astsal pa/rab 'byor de de bzhin te/rab 'byor legs so legs so//
2b5('bum): rab 'byor de ni ming du yang chags pa yin/mtshan mar yang chags pa yin no//gsol pa/bcom ldana'adas ji ltar na ming du yang chags shing /mtshan mar yang chags ba     
2b6('bum): lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka'+cal pa/rab 'byor 'di la rigs kyi bu 'am/rigs kyi bu mo byang chub sems dpa'i thegapa pa shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyis pa la/ming du 'dzin 
2b7('bum): tam/mtshan mar 'dzin na de ni/shes rab kyi pha rolatu phyin pa la ming dang mtshan mar 'dzin cing /shes rab kyi pha rolatu byin pa la chags pas/shes rab kyi pha rolatu   
3a1('bum): @#//phyin pa la ming dang mtshan mar 'dzin cing /shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la chags pas/shes rab kyi pha rol tu byin pa gsog tu 'byin to//shes rab kyi pha rol tu 
3a2('bum): phyin pa ring du spong ngo //rab 'byor gal te rigs kyi bu'am/rigs kyi bu mo byang chub sems dpa'i theg pa la shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa la mtshan mar 'dzin cing /de shes rab 
3a3('bum): kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la mtshan mar gzung ste/shes rab kyi pha rol tu sbyin pas rlom sems su byed na/de shes rab kyi pha rol tu byinapas rlom sems su mi byed cing /shes 
3a4('bum): rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pas la chags pas/shes rab gyi pha rol tu phyin pa gsog tu 'byin to/shes rab kyi pha rol tu 'byin pa 'di yang de ltar legs par bshad cing /legs 
3a5('bum): par bstan pa la/chags pa dang ma chags pa de dag kyang mchis pa ngo mtshar che'o//de nas tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwa ti'i bus/tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor la 'di skad ces smras so//tshe 
3a6('bum): dang ldan pa rab 'byor/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyis chags pa ni gang yin/ma chags pa ni gang yin/rab 'byor gyis smras pa/tshe dang ldan pa shA ri'i bu/'di la byang 
3a7('bum): chub sems dpa'i theg pa pa'i rigs kyi bu'am/rigs kyi bu mos thabs mi mkhas pas/gzugs stong pa zhes bya ba rab du shes na chags pa'o//tshor ba stong pa zhes bya bar 'du shes na 
3a8('bum): chags pa'o//'du shes stong pa zhes bya bar 'du zhes na chags pa'o//'du byed stong pa zhes bya bar 'du shas na chags pa'o//rnam par shes pa stong pa zhes bya bar 'du/zhes na chags 
3b1('bum): ba'o//mig stong pa zhes bya bar 'du shes na chags pa'o//rna ba stong pa zhes bya bar 'du zhes na chags pa'o//sna stong pa zhes bya bar 'du shas na chags pa'o//lce stong pa zhes bya bar 'du nyes 
3b2('bum): na chags pa'o//lus stong pa zhes bya bar 'du shis na chags pa'o//yid stong pa zhes bya bar 'du byas na chags pa'o//gzugs stong pa zhes bya bar 'dushes na chags pa'o//sgra stong 
3b3('bum): pa zhes bya bar 'du shes ni chags pa'o//dri stong pa zhes bya bar 'du ni shes na chags pa'o//ro stong pa zhes bya bar 'du des na chags pa'o//reg stong pa zhes bya bar 'do shes na chags 
3b4('bum): pa'o//chos stong pa zhes bya bar 'du/shes na chags pa'o//mig gi rnam par shes pa stong pa zhes bya bar 'du ris na chags pa'o//rna ba'i rnam par shes pa stong pa'o//zhes bya bar 'du shes 
3b5('bum): na chags pa'o//sna'i rnam par shes pa stong ba zhes bya bar 'du shes na chags pa'o//lce'i rnam par shes pa stong pa'i zhes bya bar 'du shes na/chags pa'o//lus kyi rnam par shes pa 
3b6('bum): stong pa zhes bya bar 'du shes na lags pa'o//yid kyi rnam par shes pa stong pa/zhes bya bar 'du shes na chogs pa'o//mig gi 'dus te reg pa stong pa zhes bya bar 
3b7('bum): 'du shes na chags pa'o/lus kyi 'dus te reg pa stong ba zhes bya bar 'do shes na/chags pa'o//yid kyi 'dus te reg pa stong pa/zhes bya bar brdung shes na chags pa'o//lI li 'dus te reg pa stong pa'i zhes bya bar/'du 
4a1('bum): @#//pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba stong pa zhes bya bar 'du shes na chags pa'o//rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba stong ba zhes bya bar 'du shes na cags pa'o//sna'i 
4a2('bum): 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyi tshor ba stong pa zhes bya par 'du shes na chags pa'o////lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen kyis tshor ba stong pa zhes bya gar 'du shes na chags pa'o //lus kyi 'dus te 
4a3('bum): reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor pa stong pa zhes bya bar 'dushes na chags pa'o//yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyisa tshor ba stong pa zhes byabar 'du shes na chags pa 'o//sa'i khams stong pa 
4a4('bum): zhes bya bar 'du shes na cha gas pa'o//chu'i khams stong pa zhes bya bar 'du zhes na chags pa'o//me'i khams stong pa zhes bya par 'dushes na chags pa'o //rlung gi khams stong pa 
4a5('bum): zhes bya par 'du shes na cags pa 'o//nam mkha'i khams stong pa zhes bya ba 'du shes na chags pa'o/rnam par shes pa'i khams stong pa zhes bya phar 'du shes na chags pa'o
4a6('bum): /ma rig pa stong pa zhes bya bar gyur shes na chags pa'o//'du byed stong pa zhes bya bar 'du/shes na chags pa'o//rnam par shes pa stong pa zhes bya bar bar 'du shes na chags pa'o//ming dang 
4a7('bum): gzugs stong pa zhes bya ba mang du shes na chags pa'o/skye mched drug stong pa zhes bya bar 'dud shes na chags pa'o/reg pa stong pa zhes bya ba 'dud shes na chags pa 'o//tshor ba stong pa 
4a8('bum): zhes bya bar 'ang du shes na chags pa'o//sred pa stong pa zhes bya bar 'dushes na chags pa'o//len pa stong pa zhes bya bar 'ang du shes na/chags pa'o//srid pa stong pa zhes bya bar du shes na 
4b1('bum): chags pa'o//skye ba stong pa zhes bya bar 'du shes na chags pa'o//rga shi stong pa zhes bya bar 'du zhes na chags pa'o//sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa stong pa zhes bya bar 'du shas na chags pa'o//
4b2('bum): tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa stong ba zhes bya bar 'dus shes na chags pa'o//bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa steng pa zhes bya bar 'du she na chags pa'o//brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin 
4b3('bum): pa stong pa zhes bya bar 'dushes na chags pa'o//bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa stong pa'i zhes bya bar 'du shes na/chags pa'o//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ston pa zhes bya bar 'du shes 
4b4('bum): na chags pa'o//nang stong pa nyid stong pa zhes bya bar 'du shis na chags pa'o//phyi stong pa nyid stong pa zhes bye bar 'du shes na chags 
4b5('bum): pa'o//byin pa stong pa nyid stong pa zhes pa yin no//de ni 'du shes na chags pa'o////de ni stong pa nyid stong pa zhes//'du shes na chags pa'o//de ni stong pa nyid stong ba zhes bya ba 'du shes na chags pa'o/da ni stong pa nyid stong 
4b6('bum): pa nyid stong pa zhes bya ba 'du shes na gang la chags pa'o//thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid stong ba zhesabya bar 'du shes na chags pa'o/dor ba med pa stong pa nyid stong pa la zhes bya bar 'du shes na chags
4b7('bum): rang bzhin stong pa nyid stong pa zhesabya bar 'du shes na gang la'o//chos thams cad stong pa nyid stong pa zhes ba bar 'du shes na chags ba'o//rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid stong pa zhe bya bar 'du shes 
5a1('bum): @#//na chags pa'o//mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid stong pa zhes bya bar 'du shes na chags pa'o//dngos po med pa stong pa nyid stong pa shes bya bar 'du shes na chag pa'o//
5a2('bum): ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid stong pa zhes bar 'du shes na chags ba'o//dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid stod pa zhes bya bar 'du shesan chags pa'o//dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams stong pa 
5a3('bum): zhes bya bar 'du shes na lags pa'o//yang dag par spong ba rnams stong pa zhes bya bar 'dushes na chags pa'o//rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams stong pa zhes bya ba 'du shes na chags 
5a4('bum): pa'o//dbang po rnams stong pa zhes bya ba' 'du shes na chags pa'o//stobs rnams stong pa zhes bya ba'i 'du shes na chags pa'o/byang chub kyi yan lag rnams stong pa zhes bya ba
5a5('bum): 'du shes na chags pa'o/'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa stong pa zhes bya bar 'du zhes na chags pa'o//'phags pa'i bden pa rnams stong pa zhes ba bar 'du shes na 
5a6('bum): chags pa'o//bsam gtan rnams stong pa zhes bya bang 'du shes na chags pa'o/o/tshad med pa rnams stong pa zhes byar 'du shes na chags pa'o/
5b1('bum): pa zhes bya bar 'du shes na chags pa'o//ting nge 'dzin rnams stong pa zhes bya bar 'du shes nas chags pa'o//gzungs kyi sgo rnams stong pa zhes bya ba 'du shes na chags pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa'i 
5b2('bum): stobs rnams stong pa zhes bya ba'ang 'du shes na chags pa'o/mi 'jigs pa rnams stong pa zhes bya ba'i 'du shes na chags pa'o/so so yang dag par rig pa rnams stong pa zhes bya par 'du shes 
5b3('bum): chags pa'o//byams pa chen po stong pa zhes bya bar 'du zhes na chags pa'o//snying rje chen po stong pa zhes bya par 'du zhes na chags pa'o//sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad stong pa zhes bya bar 
5b4('bum): shes na chags pa'o//rgyun du zhugs pa'i 'bras bu stong pa zhes bya bar 'du shis na chags pa'o//lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu stong pa zhes pa bya bar 'du shes na chag pa'o//phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras 
5b5('bum): bu stong pa zhes bya bar 'dushes na chags pa'o//dgra bcom pa nyid stong pa zhes bya bar 'du shes na chags pa'o//rang byang chub stong pa zhes bya bar 'du shas na chags pa'o//lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid 
5b6('bum): stong pa zhes bya bar 'du she na chags pa'o//rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid stong pa zhes bya par 'du shas na chags pa'o//tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwa ti'i bu/gzhan yang byang chub sems dpa'i theg 
5b7('bum): pa pa'i rigs kyi bu laM/rigs kyi bu mo thabs mi mkhas pa/'das pa'i chos rnams la 'das pa'i chos shes bya bar 'du shes na chagags pa'o//ma 'ongs pa'i chos rnams lam 'ongs pa'i chos shes 
5b8('bum): bya bar 'du shes na chags ba'o//da ltar byung ba'i chos rnams la da ltar byung ba'i chos shes bya bar 'du shas na cha gas pa'o//tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dA ti'i bu gzhan par byang chub sems dpa'i 
6a1('bum): @#//theg pa pa'i rigs kyi bu 'am/rigs kyi bu mo dmigs pa'i tshul du sems dang po bskyed pa nye bar bzung ba nas/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod na chags 
6a2('bum): pa'o//tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyodan chags pa'o//bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa las spyod na chags pa'o//brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa las spyad na chags pa'o//
6a3('bum): bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa la sbyod na chags pa'o//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la/spyod na chags pa'o//nang stong pa nyid la spyod na chags pa'i//phyi stong pa nyid la spyod na la chags pa'o//
6a4('bum): phyi nang stong pa nyid la spyod na/chags pa'o//stong pa nyid stong pa nyid la spyod na 'chags pa'o//chen po stong pa nyid la spyod na tshags pa'o//don dam pa stong pa nyid la spyod na thags pa'o//'dus byas 
6a5('bum): stong pa nyid la spyod na bags pa'o//'dus ma byas stong pa nyid la spyod na tshogs pa'o//mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid la spyod la chags pa'o//thog ma dang tham med pa stong pa nyid la spyod 
6a6('bum): na chags pa'o//dor ba med pa stong pa nyid la sbyod na chags pa'o/rang bzhin stong pa nyid la spyod na chogs pa'o//chos thams cad stong pa nyid la spyod na cags pa'o//rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa 
6a7('bum): nyid la spyod na chags pa'o//mi dmigs pa'i stong pa nyid la spyod na cha gas pa'o//dngos po med pa stong pa nyid la dpyod na chags pa'o//ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid la spyod da chags pa'o//dngos po medapa'i 
6a8('bum): ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid la/spyod na chags pa'o//dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams la spyod na chags pa'o/yang dag par spong ba rnams la spyod na chags ba'o//rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams la spyod na chag 
6b1('bum): pa'o//dbang po rnams la spyod na chags pa'o//stobs rnams la spyod na chags ba'o//byang chub kyi yan lag rnams la spyod na chags la'o//'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa la spyod na 
6b2('bum): chags pa'o//'phags pa'i bden pa rnams la spyod na chags ni/bsam gtan rnams la spyod na chags so//tshad med pa rnams la spyod na chag ba'o//gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug 
6b3('bum): pa rnams la spyod na chags po'o//rnam par thar pa rnams la spyod na chags//mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams la spyod na chagasapa'o//rnam par thar ba'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /
6b4('bum): mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa med pa la spyod na chags pa'o/mngon par shes pa rnams la spyod na chags
6b5('bum): pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs rnams la spyod na chag pa'o//mi 'jigs pa rnams la spyod na cha gas pa'o//so so yang dag par rig pa rnams la spyod na chag
6b6('bum): sa pa'o//byis pa chen po la spyod na chags pa'o//snying rje chen po la spyod na chag pa'o//sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad la spyod na chags pa'o/rgyan tu zhugs pa'i 'bras bu la spyod na chags pa'o/lan 
6b7('bum): cig phyir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu la spyod na cha gas pa'o//phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bu la spyo na chags pa'o//dgra bcom pa nyid la spyod na chags pa'o//rang byang chub la spyod na chags pa'o/'am gyi rnam par 
6b8('bum): shes pa nyid la spyod na chags//rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid la spyod na chags ba'o//tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwa ti'i bu gang yang 'di skad du/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 
7a1('bum): @#//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa rnams kyis chags pa med pa gang zhes zer ba ni/tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwa ti'i bu/'di la byang chub sems dpa' sems 
7a2('bum): dpa' chen po thabs mkhas pa shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa la spyod pa ni gzugs la gzugs shes bya bar 'du shes pa med do//tshor ba la tshor ba zhes bya bar 'du shes pa med/
7a3('bum): 'du shes la 'du shes zhes bya bar 'du shes pa mang ngo//'du byed la 'du byed ces bya bar 'du shes pa medado//rnam par shes pa la rnam par shes pa zhes bya bar 'du shes pa mid do//
7a4('bum): 'das pa'i chos rnams la 'das pa'i chos shes bya bar 'du shes ba med do//ma 'ongs pa'i chos rnams la ma 'ongs pa'i chos shes bya bar nges du shes pa med do//da ltar byung ba'i chos 
7a5('bum): rnams la da ltar byung ba'i chos shes bya bar 'du she ba med do//tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwati'i bu byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po thabs mkhasapa shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin 
7a6('bum): pa la spyod pa ni 'di snyam du/bdag gis sbyin pa zhig sbyin no//'di la sbyin pa zhig sbyin no//sbyin pa 'di lta bu zhig sbyin na//bdag gis ni tshul khrims shig bsrung ngo//tshul khrims 
7a7('bum): 'di lta bu zhig bsrung ngo zhe'am/bdag gis ni bzod pa zhig bsgom mo//bzod pa 'di lta bu zhig bsgom mo zhe'am/bdag gis ni brtson 'grus zhig brtsam mo//brtson 'grus 'di lta 
7a8('bum): bu zhig brtsam mo zhe'am/bdag gis na bsam gtan la mnyam par gzhag go/bsam gtan 'di lta bu zhig mnyam par gzhag go zhe'am/bdag gis ni shes rab zhig bsgom 
7b1('bum): mo//shes rab 'di lta bu zhig bsgom mo zhe 'am/bdag gis ni bsod nams zhig bskyed do//bsod nams 'di lta bu zhig bskyed do//bsod rnams 'di tsam zhig bskyed do//bdag gis 
7b2('bum): ni byang chub sems dpa'i skyon med bar chud par bya'o//bdag gis ni sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag par bya'o//bdag gis nisems can yongs su smin par bya'o zhe 'am/bdag gis 
7b3('bum): ni rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid thob par bya'o snyam du sems pa med de/tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwa ti'i bu/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po thabs mkhas pas/shes rab 
7b4('bum): kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa ni/rnam par rtog pa de lta bu thams cad nang stong pa nyid dang /phyi stong pa nyid dang /phyi nang stong pa nyid dang /stong pa nyid stong pa nyid dang /chen po stong pa nyid dang /don dam pa 
7b5('bum): stong pa nyid dang /'dus byas stong pa nyid dang /'du sam byas stong pa nyid dang /mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid dang /thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid dang /dor ba med pa stong pa nyid dang
7b6('bum): rang bzhin stong pa nyid dang /chos thams cad stong pa nyid dang /rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid dang /mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid dang /dngos po med pa stong pa nyid dang/ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid dang/
7b7('bum): dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid kyi phyir med cing mi rgyu ste/tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwa ti'i bu/de dag ni byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po thabs mkhas pas/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin 
7b8('bum): pa la spyod pa'i chags pa med pa dag go/de nas lha'i dbang po brgya byin gyis/tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor la 'di skad ces smras so//btsun pa rab 'byor rnam grangs gang gis na/byang chub sems dpa'i 
8a1('bum): @#//theg pa pa'i rigs kyi bu 'am/rigs kyi bu mo rnams kyis chags par 'gyur ba yin/de skad ces smras pa dang /tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor gyis/lha'i dbang po brgya 
8a2('bum): byin la 'di skad ces smras so//ke'u shi ka 'di la byang chub sems dpa'i theg pa pa'i rigs kyi bu dang /rigs kyi bu mos sems su 'du shes pa dang /sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin par 'du 
8a3('bum): shes pa dang /tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin par 'du shes pa dang /bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin par du shes pa dang /brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin par bdu shes pa dang /bsam gtan 
8a4('bum): gyi pha rol tu phyin par 'du zhes pa dang /shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin par 'dod shes pa dang /nang stong pa nyid du 'du shes pa dang /phyi stong pa nyid du 'du shes ba dang /phyi nang stong pa nyid du 
8a5('bum): 'du shes pa dang /stong pa nyid stong pa nyid du 'du shes dang /chen po stong pa nyid du 'du shes sa dang /don dam pa stong pa nyid du 'du shes/'dus byas stong pa nyid du 'du shes
8a6('bum): dang /'dus ma byas stong pa nyid du 'dushes pa dang /mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid du 'du zhes pa dang /thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid du 'du shis pa dang /dor ba med pa stong pa nyid du
8a7('bum): shes pa dang /rang bzhin stong pa nyid du 'du shesapa dang /chos thams cad stong pa nyid du 'du she sa pa dang /rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid du 'dushes ba dang /mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid du 'dushes
8a8('bum): pa dang /dngos po med pa stong pa nyid du ba shes pa dang /ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid du 'du+e shes pa dang /dngos po med ba'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid du'adu shes pa dang /dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams su 'du 
8b1('bum): shes pa dang /yang dag par spong ba rnams su 'du shes pa dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams su 'du shes pa dang/dbang po rnams su 'du shes pa dang/stobs rnams su 'du shes pa dang
8b2('bum): lag rnams su 'du shes pa dnga'aphags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad par 'du shes pa dang /'phags pa'i bden pa rnams su 'du shes ba dang /bsam gtan rnams su 'du shes pa dag
8b3('bum): tshad med pa rnams su 'du shes pa de dang /gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams su 'du shes pa rang /rnam par thar pa rnams su 'du shes padang /mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par
8b4('bum): 'jug pa rnams su 'du she pa dang /rnam par thar ba'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa dang smon pa med par 'du shes pa dang mngon par shes pa rnams su 'du shes pa/ting nge 
8b5('bum): 'dzin rnams su 'du shes pa dangs/gzungs kyi sgo rnams su 'du shes pa dngade bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs rnams su 'du shes ba da/mi 'jigs pa rnams su 'du shes dang /so so yang dag par
8b6('bum): rig pa rnams su 'du shes ba/dang /byams pa chen por 'du shes pa dang /snying rje chen por 'du shes pa dag /sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad du 'du shes pa dang /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
8b7('bum): 'das rnams su 'du shes par gyur te/sangs rgyas la bskyed pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba dag tu 'du shes nas/dge ba'i rtsa ba de dag mngon par bsdus te/gcig tu brtul nas/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs
8b8('bum): pa'i byang chub tu yongs su bsngo zhing /yongs su bsngo ba der yang 'du shes ba de dag ni/ko'u shi ka byang chub sems dpa'i theg pa pa'i rigs kyi bu dang /rigs kyi bu mo rnams kyi chags pa ste/chags
9a1('bum): @#//pa de dag la chags par gyur na/chags pa med pa'i shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod par mi nus so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni ko'u shi ka gzugs kyi rang bzhin 
9a2('bum): yongs su bsngo bar mi nus so//tshor ba'i rang bzhin yongs su bsngo bar mi nus swo//'du shes kyi rang bzhin yongs su bsngo par mi nus so//'du byed kyi rang bzhin yongs su 
9a3('bum): bsngo bar mi nus so//rnam par shes pa'i rang bzhin yongs su bsngos bar mi nus so//mig gi rang bzhin yongs su bsngo 
9a4('bum): bar mi nus so//rnam pa bsngo bar mi nus so//lce'i rang bzhin yongs su brjod par mi nus so//sna'i rang bzhin 
9a5('bum): yongs su sngo bar mi nus so//lce'irang bzhin yongs su bsngo dang mi nus so//lus kyi rang bzhin yongs su sngo bar mi nus 
9a6('bum): so//yid kyi rang bzhin yongs su dag par mi 'gyur ro//gzugs 
9a7('bum): kyi rang bzhin yongs su ba sngo bar mi nus so//sgra'i rang bzhin yongs su bsdo bar mi nus so//dri'i rang bzhin yongs su bsgo bar mi nus so//ro'i rang bzhin yongs 
9a8('bum): su bsngo bar mi nus so//de ni gang gi rang bzhin yongs su mthun par mi nus so//de rnam par shes 
9a9('bum): pa'i rang bzhin yongs su snga ba sngo bar mi nus so/rnam par shes pa'i rang bzhan yongs su bsngo bar mi nus so/mig gi rnam par shes pa'i rang 
9a10('bum): bzhin yongs su bsngo bar min nus so//sna'i rnam par shes pa'i rang bzhin yongs su bsngo bar 
9a11('bum): rnam par shes pa'i rang bzhini yongs su bsngo bar mi nus/yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i rang la bzhin yongs su bsngo bar ma nus so//mig gi 'dus te reg pa'i rang bzhin yongs su/bsngo bar mi 
9b1('bum): nus so//rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rang bzhin yongs su bsngo bar mi nus so//sna'i 'dus te reg pa'i rang de bzhin yongs su bsngo bar ma nus so//lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rang gzhin yongs su bsngo bar 
9b2('bum): nus so//lus kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rang ba zhin yongs su bsngo bar mi nas so//yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rangabazhin yongs su bsngo bar minus so//mig gi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshong ba'i 
9b3('bum): rang bzhin yongs su bsngo par mi nus so//rna ba'i 'dus tereg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba'i rang bzhin yongs su bsgo bar mi nus so//sna'i mdus te reg pa'i rkyen gyi sa tshor ba'i rang bzhin yod sa 
9b4('bum): su bsngo bar mi nus so/lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen kyis tshor ba'i rang bzhin yogs su bsngo bar mi nus so //lus kyi 'dus te reg ba'i rkyen gyis tshor ba'i rangabazhin yongs su bsngo
9b5('bum): bar mi nus so//yid kyi 'dus tereg pa'i rkyen gyi sa tshor pa'i rang bzhin yongs su bsngo b.rami nus so//sa'i khams kyi rang bzhin yongs su bsngos par mi nus so//chu'i khams kyi rang bzhin
9b6('bum): yongs su bsngo bar mi nus so//me'i khams kyi rang bzhin yangs su bsngo bar mi nus sa so//rlung gi khams kyi rang bzhin yong su bsngo bar mi nus so///nam mkha'i khams kyi rang bzhin
9b7('bum): yodas su bsngo bar mi nus so/ /rnam par shes pa'i khams kyi rang bzhin yodas su bsngo bar mi nus//ma rig pa'i rang bzhin yongs subasngo bar mi nus so//'du byed kyi rang bzhin yongs sub sngo
9b8('bum): bar mi nus so//rnam par shes pa'i rang bzhin yongs sum sngo bar mi nus so//ming dang gzugs kyi rang bzhin yongs sum bsngo bar mi nus so/skye ba ched drug gi rang bzhin yongs su bsdo bar 
10a1('bum): @#//mi nus so//reg pa'i rang bzhin yongs su bsngo bar mi nus so//tshor ba'i rang bzhin yongs su bsngor mi nus so//sred pa'i rang bzhin yongs su sngo bar mi nus so//sred ba'i rang bzhin yongs su
10a2('bum): nus so//len pa'i rang bzhin yongs subasngo bar mi nus so//srid pa'i rang bzhin yongs pu bsngo bar mi nus so/skye ba'i rang bzhin yongs sub sngo bar mi nus so//rga shi'i rang bzhin yongs
10a3('bum): su bsngo bar mi nus so/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'i rang bzhin yongs du bsngo bar mi nus so/tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i rang gzhin yongs su bsngo bar minus so//bzod pa'i
10a4('bum): pha rol tu phyin pa'i rang pazhin yongs su bsngo bar min nus so//brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'irang bzhin yongs su bsngo par mi nus so//bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i rangabazhin yongs
10a5('bum): su bsngo bar ma nus so//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i ngang bzhin yongs su bsngo bar ma nus so/nang stong pa nyid kyi rang bzhin yongs su ba sngo bar mi nus so/phyi stong pa nyid kyi rang bzhi na yongs su
10a6('bum): bsngo bar mi nus so/phyi nang stong pa nyid kyi rang gzhin yongs su bsngobar mi nus so//stong pa nyid stong pa nyid kyi rang bzhis yongs su bsngo bar mi nus so/chen po stong pa nyid kyi rang bzhig yongs su
10a7('bum): bsngo bar mi nus/don dam pa stong pa nyid kyi rang bzhi' yongs su bsngo bar mi nas so//'dus byas stong pa nyid kyi rang bzhing yongs su bsngo bar mi nus/'dus ma byas stong pa nyid kyi rang
10a8('bum): yongs su bsngo bar mi nus do//mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid kyi rang pazhin yongs su bsngo bar mi nu so//thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid kyi rang zhen yongs su bsngo bar min nus so//
10b1('bum): dor ba med pa stong pa nyid kyi rang bzhin yongs su bsngo bar mi nus so//rang bzhin stong pa nyid kyi rang bzhin/yongs su bsngo bar mi nus//chos thams cad stong pa nyid kyi rang bzhinyongs su bsngo 
10b2('bum): bar mi nus so//rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid kyi rang bzhina yongs su bsngo bar mi nas so//mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid kyi rang bzhin yongs su bsngo barmi nus so//dngos po med pa stong pa nyid kyi dang bzhin 
10b3('bum): yongs su bsngo bar mi nusso//ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid kyi rangbzhin yongs su bsngo bar mi nus so//dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid kyirang bzhin yongs su bsngo par mi nus so//dran pa nye bar 
10b4('bum): gzhag pa rnams kyi rang bzhin yongs su bsngo bar mi nus so//yang dag par spong ba rnams kyi rang bzhin yongs su bsngo bar mi nus so//rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams kyi rang bzhinyongs su bsngo bar mi 
10b5('bum): nus so//dbang po rnams kyi rang bzhin yong su bsngo bar mi nus so//stobs rnams kyi rang bzhin yangs su bsngo bar mi nus so////byang chub kyi yan lag rnams kyi rang bzhin yong
10b6('bum): sa su bsngo bar mi nus so//tshad med pa rnams kyi rang bzhin yangs su bsngo bar mi nus//gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams kyi rang bzhinayongs su bsngo bar mi nus //bsam gtan rnams kyi rang bzhin yong/
10b7('bum): kyi rang bzhin yongs su bsngo barmi nus so//mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug padgu'i rang bzhin yongs su bsngobarmi nus so//rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang mtshan ma med pa dang /
11a1('bum): @#//smon pa med pa'i rang bzhin yongs su bsngo bar mi nus so//mngon par shes pa rnams kyi rang bzhin yongs su bsngo par mi nus so//ting nge 'dzin rnams kyi rang bzhin yongs
11a2('bum): bsngo bar mi nus so//gzungs kyi sgo rnams kyi rang bzhin yong su bsngo bar mi nus so//de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs rnams kyi rang bzhin yongasasu bsngo bar mi nus so/mi 'jigs pa rnams 
11a3('bum): kyi rang bzhin yongs su bsdo bar mi nus so//so so yang dag par rig pa rnams kyi rang bzhin yangs su bsngo bar mi nus pa/byams pa chen po'i rang bzhin yongs su bsgo bar mi nus so//
11a4('bum): snying rje chen po'i rang bzhin yong su bsngo par mi nus so//sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi rang bzhin yongs su ba sngo bar mi nus so//rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras bu'i rang bzhin yongs
11a5('bum): su bsngo bar mi nus so/lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu'i rang bzhin yangs su bsgo bar mi nus pa/phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bu'i rang gzhin yongs su bsngo bar mi nus so//dgra bcom pa nyid kyi rang 
11a6('bum): bzhin yongs su bsngo bar minus so//rang byang chub kyi rang bzhin yongs su bsngo bar ma nus so//lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid kyi rang bzhin yongs subasngo bar mi nus so//rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid 
11a7('bum): kyi rang bzhin yongs su bsdo bar mi nus so//ko'u shi ka gzhan yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos/gzhan zhig bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu yang dag par   
11a8('bum): bstan tam/yang dag par bskul lam/yang dag par spro ba bskyed dam/yang dag par dga' bar bya na/yang dag par rjes su song ba'i yid kyis/gzhan yang dag par bstan par bya'o/yang dag
11b1('bum): par bskul bar bya'o//yang dag par spro ba bskyed par bya'o//yang dag par dga' ba bskyed par bya'o//de yang ci nas kyang /sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa'i tshe/bdag ni sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin ba la spyod do zhes 
11b2('bum): bya bar rnam par rtog par mi 'gyur ba dang /tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyo pa'i tshe/bdag ni tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyi pa la spyod do zhes bya bar rnam par rtog par myi 'gyur ba dang /
11b3('bum): bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa las spyod pa'i tshe/bdag ni bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyi pa la spyod de zhes bya bar rnam par rtogs par mi 'gyur ba dang /brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la/spyod pa'i tshe/
11b4('bum): bdag ni brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu yin pa la spyod do zhes bya ba rnam par rtog par mi 'byung ba dang /bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyodapa'i tshe/bdag ni bsam gtan gyi pha rol du phyin pa la 
11b5('bum): spyod do zhes bya bar rnam bar rtog par mi 'gyur ba dag/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la bstod pa'i tshe/bdag ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa la sbyod do zhes bya bar rnam pa rtog par mi 'gyur ba dang/nang stong 
11b6('bum): ba nyid la spyod pa'i tshe/bdag ni nang stong pa nyid la spyod do zhes bya bar nam par rtog bar mi 'gyur ba dang /phyi stong ba nyid la spyod pa'i tshe//bdag ni phyi stong ba nyid la spyod do zhes bya par rnam par rtog bar mi 'gyur
11b7('bum): phyi nang stong ba nyid la spyod pa'itshe/bdag ni phyi nang stong ba nyid la sbyod do zhes bya bar nam bar rtog bar mi 'gyur ba dag/stong ba nyid stong ba nyid la spyod pa'i/bdag ni stong pa nyid stong ba nyid la spyod
11b8('bum): zhes bya bar rnam par rtog par mi 'gyur ba dam/chen po stong ba nyid la spyod pa'i'o//bdag ni chen po stong ba nyid la sbyod do zhe bya bar rnam par rtog bar ma'i 'gyur ba dang /don dam pa stong ba nyid la 
12a1('bum): @#//spyod pa'i tshe/bdag ni don dam pa stong pa nyid la spyod do zhes bya bar rnam par rtog par mi 'gyur ba dang /'dus byas stong pa nyid la spyod pa'i tshe/bdag ni 'dus byas stong pa nyid la spyad do 
12a2('bum): zhes bya bar rnam par rtogs par mi 'gyur ba dang /'dus ma byas stong pa nyid la spyod ba'i tshe/bdag ni lus ma byas stong pa nyid la/spyod do zhes bya bar rnam bar rtog par mi 'gyur ba dag mtha' las 'das 
12a3('bum): pa stong pa nyid la spyod pa'itshe/bdag ni mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid la spyod de zhes bya bar rnam par rtogapar mi 'gyur ba dang /thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid la spyod pa yin pa'i tshe/bdag ni thog ma dang tha ma 
12a4('bum): med pa stong pa nyid la spyo do zhes bya bar rnam par rtag par mi 'gyur ba dang dor ba med pa stong pa nyid la spyad pa'i tshe/bdag ni dor ba med pa stong pa yid la spyod do zhes bya ba rnam par rtog par mi 'byung 
12a5('bum): ba dang /rang bzhin stong pa nyid la spyod pa'i tsha/bdag ni rang bzhin stong pa nyid la/spyod do/zhes bya bar rnam par rtog par mi 'gyur ba dang chos thams cad stong pa nyid la spyod pa'i/bdag ni chos thams 
12a6('bum): cad stong pa nyid la spyod do zhes bya bar rnam par/rtog par mi 'gyur ba dang/rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid la spyod ba'i tshe/bdag ni rang gi mtshan nyid stong ba nyid la spyod do zhes bya par rnam par rtog par
12a7('bum): mi 'gyur ba dang /mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid la spyod ba'i/bdag ni mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid spyod do zhes bya bar rnams par rtog par mi 'gyur pa dang /dngos po med pa stong pa nyid la spyod pa'itshe/
12a8('bum): bdag ni dngos po med pa stong pa ni la spyod do zhes bya bar nam par rtog par mi 'gyurab dang /ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid la spyodapa'i tshe/bdag ni ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid la/spyod do zhes bya bar rnam bar rtog par
12b1('bum): mi 'gyur ba dang /dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid la spyod pa'i tshe/bdag ni dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nying stong pa nyid la spyod do zhes bya bar rnam par rtog par mi 'gyur ba dang /dran pa nyer bar gzhag pa 
12b2('bum): rnams la spyod pa'i tshe/bdag nidran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams la spyod do zhes bya bar nam par rtog par mi 'gyur pa dang /yang dag par spong ba rnams la spyod pa'i tshe/bad ga ni yang dag par spong ba rnam+sa 
12b3('bum): la spyod do zhes bya bar rnams par rtog par mi 'gyur bdang /rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams la spyod pa'i tshe//bdag ni rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnam la spyod do zhes bya bar ram par rtog par mi 'gyurab dang /dbang po 
12b4('bum): rnams la spyod pa'i tshe/bdag ni dbang po rnams la spyod do zhes bya par rnam par rtog par mi 'byung ba dang /stobs rnams la spyod pa'i tshe/de bdag ni stobs rnams la spyod do zhes bya na rnam par rtog par 
12b5('bum): mi 'gyur ba dang /byang chub kyi yan lag rnams la spyod pa'i tshe/dbang ni byang chub kyi yan lag rnam la spyod do zhes bya par nam par rtog par mi 
12b6('bum): 'gyur ba dang /'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad 
12b7('bum): pa la spyod pa'i tshe/bdag ni 'phags pa'i lam yan lagabargyad pa la spyod do zhes bya bar rnam par rtog par mi gyur ba dang /'phags pa bden pa rnams la spyod pa'i che/bdag ni 'phags pa'i bden pa rnams 
12b8('bum): la spyod do zhes bya bar rnams par rtog par mi 'gyur ba dang /bsam gtan rnams la spyod pa'i tshe/bdag gi bsam gtan rnams la spyod do zhes bya bar nam par rtog par mi 'gyur pa dang /tshad med pa rnams 
12b9('bum): la spyod pa'i tshe/bdag ni/tshad med pa rnams la spyod do zhes byabar rnam par rtog par mi 'gyurab dang /gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams la spyod pa'i tshe/bdag ni gzugs med pa'i snyoms 
13a1('bum): @#//bar 'jug pa rnams la spyod do zhes bya bar rnam par rtog par mi 'gyur ba dang /rnam par thar pa brgyad la spyod pa'i tshe/bdag ni rnam par thar pa brgyad la sbyod do zhes bya 
13a2('bum): bar rnam par rtog par mi rgyur ba dang /mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa dgu la spyod pa'i tshe/bdag nimathar gyis gnas pa'i snyom par 'jug pa dgu la sbyod do zhes bya 
13a3('bum): ba rnam par rtog par mi 'byung ba dang /rnam par thar ba'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa med pa la spyod pa'i tshe/bdag gi rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang mtshan ma 
13a4('bum): med pa dang /smon pa medapa la spyod do zhes bya bar rnal par rtog par mi 'gyur pa dang /mngon par shes pa rnams la spyod pa'i tshe/bdag mi mngon par shes pa rnams las spyod do zhes bya bar rnams pa 
13a5('bum): rtog par mi 'gyur ba dang/ting nge 'dzin rnams la spyod pa'i tshe//bdag ni ting nge 'dzin rnams la spyo do zhes bya bar rnam par rtog par mi 'gyur ba dang /gzungs kyi sgo rnams la spyod pa'i tshe/mdag ni 
13a6('bum): gzungs kyi sgo rnams la sbyod do zhes bya bar rnam par rtag par mi 'gyur ba dang de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs rnams la spyod pa'i tshe/dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i stong pas rnams la spyod 
13a7('bum): do zhes bya bar rnam par rteg par mi 'gyur ba dang mi 'jigs pa rnams la spyod pa'i che/bdag ni mi 'jigs pa rnams la sbyod do zhes byabar rnam par rtog par mi'agyur ba dang /so so yang dag 
13a8('bum): bar rig pa rnams la spyod pa'i tshe bdag ni so so yang dag par rig pa rnams la spyod do zhes bya bar rnam bar rtog par mi 'gyur pa dang /byams pa chen po la spyod pa'i tshe/bdag ni byams pa chen po la spyod
13b1('bum): do zhes bya bar rnam par rtog par mi 'gyur ba dang /snying rje chen po la spyod pa'i tshe/bdag ni snying rje chen po la spyod do zhes bya bar rnam par rto par mi 'gyur ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad 
13b2('bum): la spyod pa'i tshe/bdag nis sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dresapa bco brgyad la spyod do zhes bya bar nam par rtog par mi 'gyur pa dang /rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras bu la spyod pa'i tshe/bdag na rgyun tu 
13b3('bum): zhugs pa'i 'bras bu la spyo do zhes bya bar rnam par rog par mi 'gyur ba dang lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu la spyod pa'i che/bdag ni lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i 'gras bu la spyod do zhes bya 
13b4('bum): bar rnam par rtog par mi'agyur ba dang /phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bu la spyo da pa'i tshe/bdag ni phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bul spyod do zhes bya bar ranam par rtog par mi 'gyung ba dang /dgra bcom pa 
13b5('bum): nyid la spyod pa'i tshe/bdag ga ni dgra bcom pa nyid la spyod do zhes bya b.rarnam par rtog par mi 'gyurab dang /rang byang chub la spyod pa'i tshe/bdag ni rang byang chub la spyod do zhes bya bar rnam par
13b6('bum): 'gyur ba dang /lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid la spyod pa'i tshe/bad ga ni lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nying la spyod do zhes bya bar nam par rtog par mi 'gyur ba dang/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid la 
13b7('bum): spyod pa'i tshe/bdag ni rnam pa thams cad mkhyen ba nyid la spyod do zhes bya par rnam par rtog par mi 'gyu ra ba de ltar yang dag par bstan par bya'o//yang dag par bskul bar bya'o//yang dag par spro ba
13b8('bum): bskyed par bya'o//de ltar spyod pa'i rigs kyi bu 'am/rigs kyi bu mos gzhan bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu yang dag par bstan parabya'o//yang dag par bsgul bar bya'o//
14a1('bum): @#//yang dag par spro ba bskyed par bya'o//yang dag par dga' ba bskyed par bya'o//de yang dag par bstan pa de dang /yang dag par bskul ba de dang /yang dag par spro ba bskyed bar bya ba 
14a2('bum): de dang /yang dag par dga' ba bskye bar bya ba des/gzhan yang dag par ston cing /yang dag par bskul pa dang /yang dag par sprob bskyed cing /yang dag par dga' par byed pa'i tshe/
14a3('bum): rma 'byung bar mi 'gyur zhing /sangs rgyas kyis gnang ba'i yang dag par bskul bas yang dag par bskul ba yin te/de ltar na rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo des chags pa'i mtha' thams cad yongs su 
14a4('bum): spangs pa yin no////shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa stong phrag brgya pa/bam po brgya dgu bcu pa/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor la legs so zhes bya ba gnang ste/rab 'byor

20b5('bum): sdug ces bya bar mi spyod da/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa la spyod do//de ci'i pyir zhe na/gang rtag pa'am/mi rtag ba'am/bde ba'am sdug bsnga ba'am/bdag gam bdag ga med pa'am/sdug 
20b6('bum): pa'am mi sdug par 'gyur pa de lta bu'i yid med do//gal te gzugs la mi spyod na/shes rabakyi pha rol tu phyin pa la sbyod do//gal te gzugs rtag ces bye ba'am mi rtag ces bya bar mi sbyod 
20b7('bum): na shes rab kyi pha rolatu phyinab la spyod do//galas te gzugs bde zhes bya ba'am/sdugabasngal zhes bya bar mi spyod na/shes rab kyi ba rolatu phyin pa la spyod pas gal te gzugs bdag ces bya ba'am/bdag med 
20b8('bum): ces bya bar mi spyod na/she sa rab kyi pha rolatu phyinapa la spyod do//gal te gzugs sdug ces bya ba'am/mi sdug ces bya bar mi spyod na//shes rab kyi pha rolatu phyin pa la spyod do//gal te ba ni gzugs bdag ces bya ba 'am/bdag med 
21a1('bum): @#//gang rtag pa'am mi rtag pa'am/bde ba'am sdug bsngal ba'am/bdag gam bdag med pa'am/sdug pa'am mi sdug par 'gyur ba de lta bu'i gzugs med do//

31b6('bum): par 'gyur ba de lta bu'i bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa med do//gal te brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la mi spyod na/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa las spyod do//gal te brtson 'gru sa kyi pha rol tu phyin pa rtag ces

31b8('bum): pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod/gal te brtson 'grus kyi pha ral tu phyin pa bdag ces bya ba'am/bdag med ces bya bar mi spyod na/shes/rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pas la spyod do//gal te brtson/'grus 
32a1('bum): @#//kyi pha rol tu phyin pa sdug ces bya ba 'am/mi sdug ces bya bar mi spyod na/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/gang rtag pa 'am mi rtag pa 'am/bde ba 'am 
32a2('bum): sdug bsngal ba 'am/bdag gam bdag med pa 'am/sdug pa 'am mi sdug par 'gyur ba de lta bu'i brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa med do//gal te bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa la mi spyod na/

32a4('bum): te bsam gtan gyi pha rol du phyin pa bde zhes bya ba 'am/sdug bsngal zhes bya bar mi spyod na/shesar ba kyi pha rol tu phyin pa las spyod do//gal te bsam ma gtan gyi pha rol tu phyi na pa bdag

32b1('bum): pa la spyod do//gal te shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa bdag ces bya ba 'am/bdag med ces bya bar mi spyod na/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa la spyod do//gal te she rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pas sdug ces

153b2('bum): pa nyid ni khams gsum nas 'byung ba med do//rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du gnas pa med do//tshor ba stong pa nyid ni/khams gsum nas 'byung ba mid do//rnam pa thams can mkhyen pa nyid du gnas ba med 
153b3('bum): do//'du shes stong pa nyid ni khams gsum nasa'abyung ba med do//rnam ba thams cad mkhyen pa nyid do gnas pa med do//'du byed stong pa nyid ni khams gsam nas 'byung ba med do/rnam pa thams cad 
153b4('bum): mkhyen pa nyid du gnas pa mid do//rnam par shes pa stong pa yin ni khams gsum nas 'byungab med do//rnam pa thamasacad mkhyen pa nyid du gnasapa med do//de ci'i phyir zhe na 'di ltar gzugs su gzugs 
153b5('bum): kyis stong pa'i phyir ro//tshor ba tshor bas stong pa'i phyir ro//'du shes 'du shes kyis stong pa'i phyir ro/'du byed 'du byed kyis stong pa'i phyir ro/rnam par shes pa rnam par shes pas stong pa'i phyir ro//

177b3('bum): lag rnams la mi sbyor mi byed pa de'i tshe/byang chub kyi yan lag rnam la brtson par byed do//'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa la mi sbyor mi 'byed de/gang gi tshe 'phags pa'i lam 
177b4('bum): yan lag brgyad pa la mi spyor mi 'byed pa de'i tshe/'phags pa'i lam yan lagabargyad pa la brtson par byed do/'phags pa'i bden pa rnams la mi sbyor mi 'byeng de/gang gi tshe 'phags 
177b5('bum): pa'i bden pa rnams la mi spyor mi 'byed pa da'i tshe/'phags pa'i bden pa rnal la brtson par byed do/bsam gtan rnams la mi sbyor mi 'byid de/gang gi tshe bsam gtan rnams 
177b6('bum): la mi sbyor mi 'byed pa de'i tshe/bsam gtan rnams la brtson par byed do//tshad med pa rnams la mi sbyor mi byedade/gang gi tshe tshad med pa rnams la mi spyor mi byed pa de'i tshe/
177b7('bum): tshad med pa rnams la brtson par byed/gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams la mi sbyor mi 'byaid de/gang gi tshe gzugs med pa'i snyoms pa 'jug pa rnams la mi spyor 
177b8('bum): mi 'byed pa de'i tshe/gzugs med pa'i snyomasapar 'jug pa rnams la brtson par byaid do//rnam par thar pa brgyad la mi sbyor mi 'byed de/gang gi tshe rnam par thar pa brgyad las mi sbyor mi 
178a1('bum): @#//'byed pa de'i tshe/rnam par thar pa brgyad la brtson par byed do//mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa dgu la mi sbyor mi 'byed de/gang gi tshe mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms? par 
178a2('bum): 'jug pa dgu la mi sbyor ma 'byed pa de'i tshe/mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyom par 'jug pa dgu la brtson par byed do//rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa med pa la mi sbyor 
178a3('bum): mi 'byed da/gang gi tshe rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong ba nyid dang /mtshan ma medapa dang /smon pa med ba la mi sbyor mi 'byed pa de'i tshe/rnam pa thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid/mtshan ma med pa dang /

178b7('bum): dpa' chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa la spyod pa'i tshe/gzugs sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//gzugs phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so/gzugs dbus su mi dmigs so//shes rab

184a6('bum): rnams dbus su mi dmigasaso//shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa la spyod pa'i tshe/rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams sngon gyi mthar med migs so//rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnam phyi ma'i 
184a7('bum): mthar mi dmigs so//rdzu 'phrul gyi rkad pa rnams dbus su mi da migs so//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi ba la spyod pa'i tshe/dbang po rnams sngon gyi mthar mi da migs so//dbang 
184a8('bum): po rnams phyi ma'i mthar mi dmig sa so//dbang po rnams dbus su mi dmig so//shes rab kyi pha rolatu phyin pa la spyod pa'i tshe/stobs rnams sngon gyi mthar midamigs so//
184b1('bum): stobs rnams phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//stobs rnams dbus su mi dmigs so//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa'i tshe/byang chub kyi yan lag rnams sngon gyi 
184b2('bum): mthar mi dmigs so//byang chub kyi yan lag rnam phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs sa so//byang chub kyi yan la rnams dbus su mi dmig sa so//shes rab kyi pha ro la tu phyin pa 
184b3('bum): la spyod pa'i tshe/'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//'phags pa'i lam yan la brgyad pa phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs /so//'phags 
184b4('bum): pa'i lam yan lag brgyad ba dbus su mi dmigs so //shes rab kyi pha rol tuphyin pa la spyod pa'i tshe/'phags pa bden pa rnams sngon gyi mthar mi dmig sa so//'phags 
184b5('bum): pa'i bden pa rnams phyi ma'i mthar mi migs sa//'phags pa'i bden pa rnams dbus su mi dmigs//shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa la spyod pa'i tshe bsam gtan rnams 
184b6('bum): sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs sa //bsam gtan rnams phyi ma'i mthar mi dmig sa so//bsam gtan rnams dbus su mi dmig/sa so//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa'i tshe/
184b7('bum): tshad med pa rnams sngon gyi mthar yi dmigs sa//tshad med pa rnams phyi ma'i mthar ma dmigs so//tshad med pa rnams dbus su mi da migs so//shes rab kyi pha ro la tu phyin pa la 
184b8('bum): spyod pa'i tshe/gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams sngon gyi mthar dmigs so//gzugs med pa'i snyoms bar 'jug pa rnams phyi ma'i mthar yi dmigs so//
185a1('bum): @#//gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams dbus su mi dmigs so//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa'i tshe//rnam par thar pa brgyad sngon gyi mthar mi 
185a2('bum): dmigs so//rnam par thar pa brgya phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//rnam par tharapa brgyad dbus su mi dmigs so//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa la spyod pa'i tshe/mthar gyis gnas 
185a3('bum): pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa dgu sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//mthar gyi gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa pa dgu phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs sa so//mthar gyi gnas pa'i snyom par 'jug

185a8('bum): ting nge 'dzin rnams phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigasaso//ting nge 'dzin rnams dbus su mi dmigs so/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyen pa la spyod pa'i tshe/gzungs kyi sgo rnams sngon 
185b1('bum): gyi mthar mi dmigs so//gzungs kyi sgo rnams phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//gzungs kyi sgo rnams dbus su mi dmigs so//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa'i tshe/
